Alessio Bortot                                                                   The System of Declinations of The Sun is the angular distance between the plane of the
                                                                                                                                           Student : Ho Trong Nhan - 294042                                                                            equator and the Sun's declinations, representing the sun's path concerning the earth's
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       system. In sundials, two specific dates are always shown. Those are June 21 (summer
                                                                                                                                       The sun rises The sun goes down                                                                                 solstice) and December 21 (winter solstice). These dates coincide with the Sun's position
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       at its highest and lowest points above the equator, resulting in maximum and minimum
                                                                                                                                       June 22 - Sum-                                                          Entry into Cancer                       daylight hours. The Sun's declination undergoes variation throughout the day, yielding
                                                                                                                                        mer Solstice                                                                                                   a curvilinear trajectory traced by the luminous point from sunrise to sunset, exhibiting
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       a hyperbolic branch. The hyperbolic line for the summer solstice (June 22) is closest to
                                                                                                                                   May 21 - Gemini                                                               Leo - July 23                         the window; that winter (December 23) is the furthest. The bright spot within a year is
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       always between these two curves. The representation of the progression of time in the
                                                                                                                                   April 21 - Taurus                                                           Virgo - August 23                       celestial sphere involves the utilization of the Zodiac. The Zodiac encompasses a belt
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       defined by two parallel circles, positioned at an angular separation of 8°30' on either side
                                                                                                                                   March 21 - Aries                                                    Libra - September 23                            of the ecliptic.

ed to Father Bonfa's use of a trial-and-er-
ror approach during the design process.
The edited lines on this wall could still be
visible to the naked eye after a hundred
years in less than 100 square meters.

This demonstrates that the author of
this dial invested his time in calculating
methods. This work is equivalent to an
efficient scientific study, and the author
has always prioritized correctness. This
article proves Jesuit Father Jean Bonfa's
skill in mathematics and dedication.

Student : Ho Trong Nhan - 294042               The System of Declinations of the Sun can be identified by light
                                               cones. The plane at the equator is the 0° point - reference origin. The
                                               other cones inclined to the equator at: 11, 20, and 23 degrees. The
                                               lines of the Sun's declinations inside the reflective sundial are the in-
                                               tersection of the room surfaces with the light cones.
Università Iuav di Venezia                      The Geometrical Relationship Is Employed to Gener-                         The Invisible Gem of Horloge Solaire
Master's Degrees in Architecture and building   ate the French Hour System Inside the Inner Space                                                   Du Lycée Stendhal
engineering-architecture 2022-2023                                                                                                                          Grenoble (FR)
Supervisor : Professor. Agostino De Rosa                           of a Reflected Sundial.
Co-Supervisor : Professor. Alessio Bortot
Student : Ho Trong Nhan - 294042               French hour lines, formed through the intersection of the celestial
                                               sphere and planes of light. These light planes are then rotated around
                                               the north pole in multiples of 15 degrees, starting from the meridian
                                               plane that represents 12:00. The astronomical hour lines, also known
                                               as French hours, are established through the intersection between
                                               the interior surfaces and the planes of light.

Student : Ho Trong Nhan - 294042               The Twelve Astrological Houses, also known as the Celestial Houses,
                                               represent a fundamental concept within astrology. The projection of
                                               the Celestial Houses onto the interior space can be achieved by de-
                                               lineating their presence on a celestial sphere. The generation of these
                                               houses involves using a series of planes, each characterized by a rota-
                                               tion angle of 30° and a rotation axis aligned towards the north pole.
